# Real, third-party "safe/benign" domains - NOT from UT1 and NOT
# Philippines-specific, unlike the other raw/ files. Source: DNSFilter
# (a real DNS security company), github.com/DNSFilter/topdomains,
# lists/2023-06/TopDomains_2023-06_250k.csv - domains ranked by actual
# real-world DNS query volume ("domains seen, ranked by number of
# queries", min. 20 distinct organizations per vote, generated monthly).
# This is the top 20,000 of that real 250,000-domain list, truncated for
# mobile-asset/training-time practicality (same reasoning as excluding
# UT1's full 4.6M adult category elsewhere in this project).
#
# WHY THIS EXISTS: the first version of category_classifier trained ONLY
# on harmful categories (gambling/drugs/dangerous_material/adult) with no
# negative/benign class at all - it had literally never seen a normal
# site during training, so it was forced to guess one of 4 harmful labels
# for EVERY domain, including totally unrelated ones. Live testing
# confirmed this: slack.com, trello.com, duolingo.com, amazon.com and
# other clearly benign sites were being predicted as "gambling" with high
# confidence. Adding this real "safe" class is the actual fix - not a
# threshold tweak - see train_category_classifier.py's evaluation output
# for before/after false-positive numbers on held-out safe domains.
#
# Fetched (via curl) August 2026:
#   https://raw.githubusercontent.com/DNSFilter/topdomains/main/lists/2023-06/TopDomains_2023-06_250k.csv
